path; an ordinary atlas selection, including one crossing logical trim, can instead submit
the same two transformed, vertex-coloured triangles on GPU. The authored rectangle defines
geometry and colour interpolation while the whole packed page defines filtered sampling.
The selected-source and colour variants preserve ordered CPU fallbacks for runtime sprites,
nondefault blend and other unqualified states. A first-generation cardinal source edge exactly on a pixel
centre also falls back: its incremental fixed-point CPU row walk can choose a different texel
from an independently sampled GPU fragment. These are bounded ordinary-scene qualifications,
not a claim that arbitrary surface, shader, GUI or skeletal draws are resident.
Ordinary magnified `draw_sprite_stretched` atlas draws in first-generation and modern-layer
policies have a separate bounded filtered path:
it uses the CPU leaf's floored destination origin and rounded extent, preserves the full logical
cell around authored trim, and returns transparent samples only on authored margin sides.
It requires a proven opaque application target, bilinear sampling, positive magnification on
both axes and physical atlas space for the full logical quad. Transparent targets, special
blend state, out-of-atlas logical cells and deep minification retain ordered CPU execution.
The full-target coarse-mip effect has its own qualified rule below; it is not an ordinary
bilinear magnification. This qualification does not cover nine-slice or surfaces.
Ordinary non-full-target minification and mixed-axis stretches use a separate bounded box-filter
quad: each destination pixel averages the same integer source box as the CPU, excluding authored
trim texels from both the sum and divisor. The device checkpoint preserves the exact result
without a context. The first-generation and modern-layer paths accept up to eight covered
source texels per axis (64 taps), a proven opaque destination and ordinary blend state, with
or without global interpolation. Deeper boxes remain ordered CPU fallbacks. The full-target
coarse-mip veil is prepared and composed by a separate effect rule, not this per-pixel box sample.
The public `draw_sprite_pos` entry can keep an ordinary axis-aligned atlas rectangle resident
through the existing nearest or bilinear cell preparation. Its complete, cropped, trimmed,
binary-alpha and filtered/intermediate-alpha forms preserve the mapped corner geometry; the
filtered first-generation and modern-layer paths retain their distinct target-alpha and
fractional-edge rules, including the modern half-camera tie. A separate checkpointed quad
keeps convex non-axis atlas pages resident for ordinary source-over: affine parallelograms use
the CPU triangles' common inverse map, while genuinely warped forms interpolate local page
coordinates across the same two triangles. Nearest and bilinear sampling, clipped edges,
authored crops, tint-free draw alpha and intermediate source alpha retain their characterized
CPU pixels. Non-convex, self-intersecting or special-blend forms remain on the ordered CPU
path. Fractional non-affine coordinates, freely rotated nearest affine edges and nearest
warps without horizontal/vertical leading edges are conservatively excluded pending exact
pixel-centre tie rules; the corresponding filtered forms are qualified. Modern-layer
intermediate-alpha draws require a proven opaque target; first-generation source-over can
also preserve a translucent target. This does not make surface, shader, GUI or skeletal
positioned draws resident.
At the P3 boundary, CPU text resolved a preceding resident sprite before writing glyph pixels.
The live P6 rules below now admit qualified packaged and versioned runtime glyphs to GPU; a
bounded P9 rule also admits filtered, reduced immutable real-font glyphs. Both preserve the
ordered CPU barrier for excluded variants. A similarly bounded first-generation
nearest background path accepts complete untrimmed pages at unit positive scale with intermediate
alpha. It checkpoints the device's source-over
result, treats only the low RGB 24 bits of a GML white tint as colour, and retains the existing
binary-alpha replay path unchanged. A resident-only background draw whose full untrimmed source
lies conservatively outside either target axis writes no pixels and does not force a CPU barrier;
this does not qualify visible mirrors or trimmed backgrounds. A terminal automatic
application-surface underlay may also use the exact current resident
image: the existing plan's integer nearest axes select the pixels, while the GL executor reads the
scene texture directly without a CPU upload. This requires an opaque, owned application surface,
no intervening GUI draw, and the guarded prototype setting. A failed or lost device reconstructs
the source from the resource journal before the existing CPU presentation writes it. Arbitrary
rotation and GUI draws over the underlay remain unqualified and use the CPU fallback. This narrow terminal path
does not establish a no-full-frame-transfer route for general gameplay.

The narrow ordinary-sprite route now copies consecutive compatible textured-quad
descriptors into one renderer-owned, bounded (2,048-operation) pending batch. It
keeps immutable atlas sources borrowed, projects each target version in draw
order, and submits at a full queue, CPU pixel borrow, target/lifecycle boundary,
or the end of the world pass. Qualified immutable sprite/text checkpoint quads may
span up to 128 distinct draw states and 64 atlas sources in one submission; other
source/state changes still close the batch. The existing
resource authority and CPU replay path still own partial device failure and
context-loss recovery. A successful full-target clear supersedes the older
replay journal only after the device accepts that clear; an unsuccessful clear
retains the older journal for fallback. A deferred resident clear projects its
next target version through the queued prefix before submission, preserving
EXACT order without a CPU pixel borrow. The neutral resource control
`resident_pending_clear_after_queued_draw` covers application, SCREEN and numbered
targets with exact replay and on-demand authority. These optimizations do not broaden the
set of qualified scene operations or change CPU raster bodies.

## Current operation support and repair-level rules

R03-SPR-REPLACEMENT-FUNCTION extends ordinary/ext nearest ONE/ZERO sprites
across Classic/Studio,SCREEN/application/explicit zero/numbered targets and
world/mapped GUI. Signed whole scales1..64,tint,source/draw alpha including
zero,cutout and channel masks retain the existing CPU point geometry. CPU
replacement also erases the authored empty box of a packed frame before its
physical crop,even when cutout discards that crop. Both packets are staged
atomically through the existing queue; missing-source texels bypass cutout.
Source alpha is copied on preserved-alpha targets and stamped opaque on
display targets. The ordinary full-sprite axis companion below extends this
same transaction to bounded fractional nearest and filtered replacement;its
qualification does not infer rotation or special-source coverage.
No destination snapshot,float-emulation or ordinary read is
introduced. Individual partial-alpha draws allow2; opaque/binary endpoints
and unchanged coverage/channels remain EXACT0. The public paired generator
has18432 correlated controls,including fractional anchors/cameras,empty frames,
disabled blending and Classic presentation interpolation. Actual-byte detached
sealing and pixel negatives are mandatory. Rotation,fractional scales,Studio
filtering,runtime/nine-slice/program/fog/3D,active phase planes and over-bound
geometry remain explicit CPU debt or their existing separately qualified
owners; this is not whole-API/game/performance qualification. The original
R03-SPR-GUI-REPLACE unit copy and its exact comparisons are preserved.

Late-Classic retained Step admits native1:1 (including its interpolation
preference without auxiliary planes) and exact doubled-nearest output
after the initial CPU-owned presentation. R03-CLASSIC-STEP-COMPOSITION keeps
physical SCREEN,logical root and explicitly selected numbered Draw images
independent. An allocated vertical sample plane does not block an existing
qualified draw on a positive numbered non-base allocation:the unchanged CPU
writer cannot update that root plane there. No sample-plane rasterization is
added. Root/application-zero/aliased-base bindings retain their phase fences;
surface-zero phase sampling and interpolated planes are not newly admitted.
The doubled-nearest fixture's former D3D12/GLES RGBA144/207/192/0 non-pass is
repaired by this image ownership;its actual-device control requires EXACT0.
The native-interpolated fixture also requires EXACT0. Its independent clear
previously left device authority over a software sprite suffix; a subsequent
GUI observation replaced that suffix with the clear. Retaining the same GPU
numbered owner across Step/Draw/GUI avoids this mixed ownership and implicit
reads without modifying the CPU raster or sampler. Doubled-interpolated root
planes, early-Classic interpolation, forced/canvas and aliased roots remain
explicit debt, not admitted by this native policy. Other unadmitted software
suffixes after independent clears still require their checked CPU barrier;
this bounded engine admission is not a general proof of every such caller.
Positive actual-host fixtures
require EXACT RGBA0,Reset and same-run state/rewind;the fake driver owns only
metadata and dispatch. No CPU raster,shader,default or state-format change.

A numbered Draw does not itself consume the separate logical root. Its completed
bytes remain resource-owned instead of being eagerly acquired for CPU composition
after every root-to-numbered handoff. Real root writers, source-zero observers,
state and controlled context destruction still acquire the current bytes through
their existing checked resource consumers. The neutral root-handoff fixtures cover
root/numbered/root/recreated-numbered transitions at native and doubled extents,
nearest and filtered, with GLES/Core dispatch checks and actual-device EXACT0
delivered pixels, runtime/RNG and same-load Reset/state/rewind controls. They do
not admit a new draw variant, sampler, root phase policy or CPU algorithm.

R03-CLASSIC-ROOT-SAMPLES qualifies bounded admission for a freshly cleared,
borrowed logical root with nearest exact2x physical output and independently
owned vertical samples. Individual textured/primitive draws retain their EXACT0
or TOLERANCE±2 rule. Raw composition interior and alpha are EXACT0;only the CPU
missing-quarter-colour final-column RGB permits2. Compatible row copies batch
by source plane without ordinary observation,destination snapshots or CPU replay.
Fringe admission checks bounds on the recovered integer texel row,retaining the
existing centre-distance tolerance when FMA reconstructs the first centre just
below zero. The neutral surface-fringe control covers row centres and adjacent
representable UVs,plus malformed/out-of-range rejection,on both graphics APIs.
Storage reuse checks both the host pointer/extent and base incarnation;replacement
or resize observes and retires the old borrow before host reallocation,with a
retryable failure leaving ownership intact. No-clear persistent roots,owned
applications,composed/aspect/canvas frames,interpolated planes and multiple
views retain their existing owners. Classic's current clear policy makes these
samples derived for the next Draw,not an additional persistent state payload.
`test_render_resources::paired_root_storage_retirement_is_transactional` and
`test_graphics_state::classic_sample_root_state_case` own lifetime,geometry,
metadata-only sizing,full serialized VM equality and same-run state checks.
Physical SCREEN clears must not replace the independent logical pair.
Atomic paired texture preparation may stage up to2048 quads for each image,
without changing the ordinary2048-quad delivery limit. An independently owned
operation array grows only when both budgets are needed. Rejecting either
image,cloning an expanded prefix or disposing an unfinished transaction must
not deliver half a public draw or alias another queue's storage.
`paired_root_preparation_keeps_each_image_budget` covers cancellation,commit,
per-image overflow and parent/child disposal;the unchanged ordinary capacity
control remains a separate boundary. The108 `large-writer` pairs cover large
authored crops across trim,signed mixed scales,clipping,source alpha,tint and
normal/add/inverse blending,with independent predecessor colours in both planes.
They retain EXACT endpoints or individual tolerance2,not a larger error budget.
The neutral root/resize/filter
fixtures exported by `test_gpu_scene_route --keep-classic-sample-root`,
`--keep-classic-sample-resize` and `--keep-classic-sample-filtered` still
require actual-driver complete-frame,Reset and fixed-capacity frontend
state/rewind evidence. The explicit-window resize preserves its authored
unscaled port and therefore keeps that composition on CPU;filtered presentation
also refuses this two-plane admission. Other positive draws remain within D2.
The paired component selector is `classic-root-samples` with six explicit
writer/clear/glyph/axis/compose/large-writer profiles. Existing rectangle,line and circle
generators additionally pair active samples without changing their old profiles.
No CPU raster,default,shader or state-format change follows from admission.

Forced-aspect owned admission preserves EXACT target placement and coverage;
each draw retains its existing EXACT or TOLERANCE±2 sampling/blending rule.
With OpenGL selected, one nonclassic, noncomposed view may use the resident
owned application when an existing declared surface canvas already selects it,
or when its full-origin view fills a same-sized forced application raster.
This reuses CPU's existing full-raster forced-view composition, not a new fit.
The resident SCREEN pass follows only a successful resident world begin and
retains the centered-GUI exclusion. A narrow room may additionally retain a
resident owned world for a declared full-width compositor under the same final
direct-or-aligned raster guard. Its independent logical-root mask uses
R03-EXTERNAL-ROOT-MASK. If admission promoted a formerly borrowed CPU world,
R03-PROMOTED-WORLD-MASK also masks the owned world before GUI composition;
an already explicit CPU application canvas keeps only the independent root mask.
Centered narrow GUI,
translated ports, live
assigned view surfaces, multiple views and ineligible contexts keep their
ordered CPU paths; those ordinary fallbacks remain unfinished GPU coverage,
not newly documented rare exceptions. No raster kernel or state schema changes.
`tests/integration/test_graphics_state.c::forced_owned_scene_case` compares
complete CPU/selected images and positively checks scene admission plus the
narrow full-width positive and centered negative. The same neutral fixture is exported by
`test_gpu_scene_route --keep-forced-owned` and `--keep-forced-narrow` for real
device comparison. Recording-driver success alone is not device pixel,
ordinary-transfer or whole-route performance qualification.

The single-view coordinator also selects the existing resident base preparation
before entering a native-sized numbered `view_surface_id` target. This applies
only to non-classic, non-composed, non-forced-aspect frames with one visible view,
matching world and surface dimensions, an active device and selected OpenGL scene
rendering. The view retains its resolved room camera and the ordinary numbered
target facade; unsupported draws still take their existing ordered CPU barrier.
After sealing, only a drawn base is immediately observed for CPU composition;
an assigned destination remains resource-owned until an actual consumer reads it.
Resized assignments and absent or ineligible contexts retain the established CPU
path. No CPU raster, shader, state schema, multiview compositor or sampling rule
changes. `tests/integration/test_graphics_state.c::resident_native_assigned_view_case`
checks admission with application drawing enabled and disabled, a displaced camera,
resized-target exclusion, exact canonical state crossing, completed-frame restoration
and context loss/recreation. Its fake driver is not real-device pixel or speed proof.

The experimental magnified full-source numbered-surface rule is TOLERANCE±2 per draw,
with the composed-image contract and remaining RGBA limitations above.
`common/gml_render_surfaces.c::resident_surface_filtered` uses the unchanged
CPU stretched rectangle's floor/round geometry, metadata-only current source views, and the
existing versioned target queue. CPU source writes, target switches, resize, free, and renderer
teardown flush before borrowed source storage can change. `gpu/opengl/gml_gpu_opengl_batch.c::hardware_surface_quad_valid`
requires ordinary source-over, full-source hardware bilinear magnification and matching opaque
written-alpha coverage; `::flush_quads` uses one RGB draw plus one alpha-only draw per compatible
batch, without destination snapshots. `gpu/opengl/gml_gpu_opengl_shaders.h::R03_SHADER_HARDWARE_FILTERED_SURFACE`
uses hardware filtering and no CPU-float emulation. The unchanged CPU comparison owner is
`cpu/gml_render_cpu_blit_internal.h::surf_bi_band`. `tests/unit/video/gpu/test_gpu.c::scene_hardware_surface_batch_case`
checks capacity, separate functions and pre-write exclusion; `tests/unit/video/renderer/test_render_resources.c::filtered_surface_pending_source_lifetime`
checks write/resize/free/target/teardown boundaries and retained retry after a pre-write refusal.
These call/lifetime checks do not themselves establish composed pixel, whole-route or performance coverage.
Application sources, reduction, mirrors, active shaders and special blend state keep their existing paths.

`common/gml_render_sprites.c::resident_filtered_atlas_page` checks finite normalised
atlas UV coordinates within [0,1] before enqueuing an axis-aligned candidate. A valid physical
source rectangle does not guarantee this after fractional coverage rounding. Unqualified
coordinates retain the ordered CPU path; they are not clamped or admitted into an immutable
batch which the device would refuse. `tests/unit/video/renderer/test_render_resources.c::resident_atlas_uv_refusal_preserves_queued_prefix`
checks all four atlas boundaries and successful completion of the preceding valid queued draw.

This is the live operation-ledger view, distinct from the P1 snapshot below.
`TEXTURED_QUAD` also carries the already-qualified two-triangle positioned and
corner-colour geometry; the separate `TEXTURED_TRIANGLES` opcode is not selected.
The terminal resident-source optimization belongs to the existing render plan,
not to a selected neutral `PRESENT_IMAGE` scene opcode. The three unselected
scene entries name their concrete CPU fallback rather than promising separate
future executors. Their qualified quad/plan alternatives remain mapped below;
classifying the fallback does not establish ordinary-route GPU residency.

| Neutral ID | Current GPU ledger | Current interpretation |
|---|---|---|
| `TEXTURED_QUAD` | `supported` | Only the bounded sprite/background/tile/glyph and opaque or binary-alpha integer surface-part/stretched rule groups below; an unqualified public draw uses an ordered CPU barrier. |
| `TEXTURED_TRIANGLES` | `fallback` | No separate selected executor. Qualified positioned/corner-colour geometry is a textured quad; unqualified positioned geometry uses the preserved CPU triangle raster. |
| `COLOURED_PRIMITIVE` | `supported` | P6 opaque flat, circle/triangle span, line, outline and particle-square pixel regions; unqualified geometry retains the ordered CPU bridge. |
| `CLEAR` | `supported` | Qualified resident target clear. |
| `COPY_IMAGE` | `supported` | Public full/partial raw copies use EXACT RGBA device blits, including clipped logical row pitches and snapshot-equivalent aliases. Unsupported context/resource/history states retain the original ordered CPU kernels; R03-P5-SURFACE-COPY owns the bounded qualification. |
| `COMPOSE_SURFACE` | `fallback` | Unqualified surface composition uses ordered CPU kernels. Qualified opaque/binary integer public part/stretched rules use `TEXTURED_QUAD`; qualified full-target program draws use `CONTENT_SHADER_DRAW`. |
| `LAYER_EFFECT` | `supported` | P7 attached room-layer tint, colourise and zoom blur plus qualified inline RGB noise use GPU arithmetic after a CPU-current capture and device checkpoint. Other filter kinds and standalone tint retain CPU fallback. This does not claim zero full-target transfer or whole-game speed. |
| `PRESENT_IMAGE` | `fallback` | The terminal resident-source optimization belongs to the existing plan; other presentation uses coherent CPU materialization. No selected neutral scene-op or zero-transfer gameplay claim. |
| `CONTENT_SHADER_DRAW` | `supported` | Qualified full-target programs preserve per-draw uniforms/samplers and distinct-target multipass without an intermediate checkpoint. Excluded target/program variants retain their mapped ordered fallback; there is no exact software shader interpreter. |
| `SOFTWARE3D_PASS` | `fallback` | Ordered CPU bridge, not accelerated 3D. |
| `RECOVER_EMPTY_SCREEN` | `supported` | Studio unit/magnified point recovery,including identity full-width recovery: EXACT global RGB-zero predicate,leading-edge placement and raw alpha cutoffs0..255;opaque/binary/no-write endpoints EXACT,individual partial RGB blending TOLERANCE+/-2. GPU reduction flags gate both colour and written-alpha passes without a result query or pixel read. Uncertified partial positive-cutout two-axis filtered magnification retains explicit prewrite refusal and its centred-bilinear CPU owner. Other variants retain their existing query/recovery owner. Resource controls prove transactional admission separately from the paired cutout/point-cutout device controls. |

`gpu/opengl/gml_gpu_opengl_resources.c::gml_gpu_opengl_scene_capabilities`
advertises the six existing selected opcode bits, plus COPY_IMAGE when framebuffer blits are available, and their neutral TEXTURE, PRIMITIVE,
SURFACE, EFFECT and SHADER families. Family bits describe the catalogue's opcodes, not
public-draw counter attribution: a SURFACE-attributed textured quad does not advertise
`COPY_IMAGE` or `COMPOSE_SURFACE` by itself. The exact masks and transactional refusal of
all three unselected opcodes are checked by
`tests/unit/video/gpu/test_gpu.c::scene_service_contract_case`, including a valid
clear prefix which must not execute before an unsupported suffix is rejected.

The anchors below use `path::symbol` relative to `src/video/renderer/`, except
`tests/` paths, which are relative to the repository root. They name actual
functions or stable shader-branch labels in source. A shader label identifies
the fragment or vertex arithmetic, not an invented executor. Rules may share
one hot quad kernel. The comparison column names a core-owned discriminator
where one exists; the same stable rule ID identifies additional device/CPU
comparisons in the external test owner's record. `pending` means the named
variant still lacks complete comparison evidence, not that registry coverage
proves pixel equivalence. The shared column owns source, geometry, eligibility
and draw-state decisions; the CPU and GPU columns own their respective pixel
rounding/sampling. Special shaders, surfaces, GUI, software3d and unqualified
blend/coverage are ordered CPU fallbacks unless a row expressly qualifies them.

The layer capture still crosses the CPU-current boundary through
`common/gml_render_effects.c::gml_render_layer_filter_begin`,
`gml_render.c::gml_render_resident_application_cpu_barrier` and, for numbered
targets, `cpu/gml_render_cpu_surfaces.c::surface_pixels`. The recognized
tint/colourise path below uploads this unfiltered capture, applies a real device
pixel rule and checkpoints the result. Unrecognized filter families continue
through `cpu/gml_render_cpu_effects.c::gml_render_cpu_layer_filter_end`; the
capture barrier comparison is
`tests/unit/video/renderer/test_render_resources.c::resident_layer_filter_capture_barrier`.
Neither path establishes zero-transfer effect composition.

`R03-PRIM-CLASSIC-ALPHA-RECT` is `TOLERANCE±2`: filled, uniform-colour rectangles
with finite partial alpha on the known-opaque borrowed early-classic SCREEN retain
their inclusive CPU raster bounds and use batched, source-free fixed source-over.
The owning-context observation witness is required. Numbered targets, unknown or
nonopaque destination alpha, outlines, gradients and other blend states retain CPU
fallback. No destination predecessor, pixel read or float-arithmetic emulation is
required during drawing; observations preserve the actual produced image. Neutral
controls are `resident_classic_alpha_rectangle_scope` and
`scene_classic_alpha_rect_batch`; the latter checks the quad-capacity boundary and
transactional rejection. CPU fallback reuses the existing rectangle raster body.

`R03-PRIM-CLASSIC-INVERSE-RECT` is `TOLERANCE±2` for RGB, with exact coverage
and opaque alpha: uniform filled rectangles using the inverse-source-colour preset
at drawing alpha 1 on the same known-opaque borrowed early-classic SCREEN use
`GL_ZERO, GL_ONE_MINUS_SRC_COLOR` for RGB and `GL_ONE, GL_ZERO` for alpha.
The existing source-free rectangle shader and inclusive CPU bounds are reused.
Compatible rectangles batch to quad capacity; changing to source-over splits the
batch. No destination snapshot, ordinary read or CPU float emulation is needed.
Numbered targets, partial drawing alpha, outlines, gradients, unknown/nonopaque
target alpha and other special states remain CPU debt, not exceptions.
`common/gml_render_primitives.c::resident_classic_alpha_rect` prepares the packet;
`common/gml_render_draw.c::gml_render_classic_inverse_rect_valid` checks its envelope;
`gpu/opengl/gml_gpu_opengl_batch.c::gml_gpu_opengl_scene_submit` executes it.
Fallback still uses `cpu/gml_render_cpu_primitives.c::gml_render_cpu_rectangle`.
Core comparisons are `resident_classic_inverse_rectangle_scope` and
`scene_classic_inverse_rect_batch`; the real-device comparison uses public
preparation, single draws, eight overlapping draws and explicitly sealed actual
pixels. Synthetic accumulation is diagnostic under the owner contract; it does
not qualify a real composed image or a whole game.

`R03-PRIM-STUDIO-ALPHA-RECT` is `TOLERANCE±2` for individual RGB, with
`EXACT` covered alpha and outside pixels. First-generation Studio filled uniform
rectangles on an ordinary SCREEN or owned application use fixed source-over RGB
and one coverage-only opaque-alpha pass per compatible quad group. The unchanged
CPU raster quantizes partial alpha; the GPU does not emulate that arithmetic.
Resolved inclusive raster bounds are clipped once, without applying world scale
again. Drawing alpha must be at least 1/256 and below one. Pending underlay
composition, modern ROOM layers, nonidentity GUI, numbered/stacked targets, gradients, outlines, custom state and shaders
remain ordinary CPU debt. No destination snapshot, drawing readback, replay or
new shader program is required. Public resource controls pair both rectangle
ports and targets; driver controls cover capacity and transactional rejection.
Synthetic eight-overlap drift is diagnostic, not real-image qualification.

`R03-PRIM-FIRSTGEN-GUI-ALPHA-RECT` separately qualifies that same executor for
first-generation identity GUI. Both logical sizes equal the target raster,
scales are one, camera and offsets are zero, and maximisation is inactive.
The already resolved raster bounds are not transformed again. Existing non-GUI
controls and the uninitialized/nonidentity GUI exclusions remain unchanged.

Typed primitive entries consume raster coordinates already resolved by the builtin
view mapping; `common/gml_render_primitives.c::resident_flat_eligible` must not
apply or reject them based on the current world scale. The flat regions, filled
spans, line boxes and outline pixels below share this rule, qualified with inactive,
unit, reduced/nonuniform, enlarged/nonuniform and reflected world state. The
explicit logical circle preparation remains `common/gml_render_primitives.c::gml_render_circle_geometry`.
Geometry budgets and ordinary opaque state restrictions are unchanged.

R03-PRIM-LINE-ALPHA-NOOP is EXACT RGBA0: the three typed integer/colour/subpixel
line ports retain a resident pass when drawing alpha is nonpositive and no enabled
destination-colour blend can write. The unchanged CPU filled-box leaf already
performs no pixel write in this state. The common predicate preserves pending
fills, stale CPU pixels, resource authority and versions without submitting an
operation or reading the destination. A no-op is not credited as a GPU draw.
Positive alpha, NaN and enabled destination-colour blends retain the existing
executor and CPU-boundary policy; visible GUI line admission is not broadened.
`tests/unit/video/renderer/test_render_resources.c::resident_line_alpha_noop`
checks all three ports, pending/committed prefixes and visible exclusions.
Device comparison R03-PRIM-LINE-ALPHA-NOOP pairs144 complete-RGBA controls
containing432 line calls, including SCREEN/owned/numbered targets and mapped GUI;
prefix/suffix bytes and explicitly sealed no-current bytes require EXACT0.

R03-PRIM-CIRCLE-ALPHA-NOOP is EXACT RGBA0: all four integer/subpixel uniform/
colour circle ports retain the same qualified nonpositive-alpha no-op, including
filled/outline and zero-radius calls. Their unchanged CPU pixel or line-box leaf
returns before writing; enabled destination-colour blends remain excluded.
The shared pure `resident_primitive_alpha_noop` predicate also retains the line
rule above. No visible circle admission, CPU body, operation count, resource
version or pending work is changed. No-op calls are not credited as GPU draws.
`tests/unit/video/renderer/test_render_resources.c::resident_circle_alpha_noop`
requires authority/pending preservation and exact positive-alpha/destination-
colour write exclusions with matched target-alpha policies. Device comparison
R03-PRIM-CIRCLE-ALPHA-NOOP pairs144 full-RGBA controls containing2304 calls across
SCREEN/owned/numbered targets and mapped GUI; visible prefix/suffix and explicitly
sealed no-current bytes require EXACT0, with zero ordinary reads or snapshots.

The bounded destination-factor surface rule may skip a fully clipped rectangle
after a CPU suffix while the witnessed scene session remains active. This observes
neither source nor destination and changes no resource version. A visible rectangle
still retains that CPU suffix; it never resumes GPU writing through this exception.

R03-PRIM-RECT-COALESCED retains the unchanged CPU outline collector and admission
for already qualified opaque rectangles. Consecutive equal-colour edge pixels
form at most four disjoint regions, with the original8192 emitted-pixel limit.
The circle, triangle, partial-alpha and special-state paths are unchanged.

R03-CLASSIC-BORROWED-VIEW also qualifies the independent SCREEN admission for an
early-Classic owned application matching the logical extent, at most one view,
interpolation off and an owning-current context witness. The existing composed,
centred and aspect-policy boundaries remain unchanged. Its automatic compositor
retains EXACT RGBA0 opaque nearest copies and the existing individual partial-alpha
TOLERANCE±2 bounds; no shader, snapshot, arithmetic or CPU executor changes.
`tests/integration/test_graphics_state.c::classic_owned_screen_admission_case`
requires two resident underlays without ordinary reads and preserves unwitnessed
fallback. `test_gpu_scene_route --keep-classic-owned-gui` exports the neutral
Draw/GUI reproducer. The existing physical owned-source/order/coverage/seal and
detachment controls remain required. Step redraw entry is separate debt; later
Classic, other extents and filtered paths retain their boundaries. No whole-game
or speed qualification is inferred.

The same early-Classic full-view borrowed world may remain resident when content
disables automatic application presentation: that flag selects composition, not
the Draw target. Independent world/SCREEN images retain their existing owners.
At the next Step, `gml_render.c::gml_render_resume_completed_screen_suffix` may
resume only the exact already-current root SCREEN, with matching allocation and
extent, an owning-current witness and no pending fill/underlay/presentation.
It preserves alpha, colour, blend and mapping metadata rather than beginning a
new pass. Later-Classic phases, filtered/aspect/canvas/composed frames, CPU-current
or changed targets and missing witnesses retain ordered fallback. Public borrowed
surface-0 sampling is not expanded by this phase admission. Existing executors
keep EXACT RGBA0 nearest opaque/binary copies and individual TOLERANCE+/-2 blends;
CPU bodies, shaders and state schemas are unchanged.
`tests/integration/test_graphics_state.c::classic_manual_application_scene_case`
pairs early native/integer-view metadata and inherited Step state on both APIs,
while retaining the later-policy negative. Its three exported scene packages
provide real-context Draw/numbered-GUI/Step, Reset and same-load state/rewind pairs.
This bounded lifetime control does not qualify new surface arithmetic or speed.
Its native package also leaves every third GUI empty. The engine's early-Classic
borrowed unit recovery retains the existing SCREEN RGB query and empty-source
observation, then shares the already-qualified white automatic compositor instead
of entering an unrelated public surface-zero CPU leaf. Default unfiltered,
unmasked, zero-camera/world-transform state only; later phases, nonunit extents
and special states retain their old recovery. This is not a zero-read claim.

R03-BG-SELECTED-FUNCTION's filtered part extension uses the shared logical-trim
intersection,hardware filtering and fixed separate blending. CPU-prepared
coverage rows own discontinuous raw cutout and bounded one-cell support,not
color or destination pixels. The normal/inverse unwrapped CPU background
leaves ignore the readable channel mask;their GPU packet likewise writes all
channels without mutating that mask. Independent world SCREEN uses opaque
covered alpha,while the qualified identity-GUI and sampled-application owners
retain their existing cheap recipes. Qualification requires the paired
part-function nearest/filtered,mask and public-binding profiles,EXACT RGBA0
endpoints,individual TOLERANCE±2,exact outside coverage,ordinary-transport
counters and actual-byte detached state controls. This bounded extension is
not a whole-function percentage;other presets,special sources and semantic
owners remain debt.

Finite negative filtered background opacity is prepared as the CPU's zero
opacity no-op without changing readable state. Above-one opacity retains its
original ordered CPU fallback unless a previously qualified owner admits it.
The public-binding bounds audit records pending controls separately and
requires exact CPU pixels without GPU credit. A normalized above-one witness
fails at its first excess3 on Mesa D3D12;the same llvmpipe control passes at2.
Native NVIDIA validation is pending;no full-control D3D12 maximum is claimed.
This isolated diagnostic is not an unavoidable precision claim
and does not authorize changing D2 or emulating float arithmetic.

Negative logical source anchors on background parts use the same CPU trim
intersection and continuous one-cell anchors as positive crops. Completely
disjoint selections remain exact no-ops. The paired source-bounds companions
cover both registry aliases,all16 readable masks and each ordinary target/
generation/filter state;other source families retain their existing fences.

The selected nearest-part companion maps all12 existing CPU preset selectors
through integer source tint and fixed separate blending. The unwrapped CPU
background modes3/5 deliberately retain their normal source-over leaf,not
the sprite/surface meanings of those factor pairs. CPU-masked presets retain
their readable channel mask;other leaves retain full writes. Replacement
uses the existing atomic empty-box/crop queue and shared axis-bound metadata
so logical margins are erased before the physical selection,including an
empty nearest sample with a nonempty rounded rectangle. Immutable nearest
BACKGROUND checkpoint packets share the compatible mixed-state queue;TILE
and other unqualified packet families do not inherit these preset extensions.
Qualification requires both public bindings,all preset/owner/policy states,
EXACT binary endpoints,individual tolerance2,independent CPU outside coverage,
negative controls and actual-byte state/transport gates. Repeated overlap
accumulation is informational under D2. Filtered nonordinary presets and the
remaining semantic/source/special owners are not qualified by this companion.

The filtered selected-part preset companion retains the same CPU meaning for
all twelve selectors. The physical hardware sampler rounds its source bytes,
then applies the existing integer tint policy before fixed separate blending.
Additive RGB prepares the CPU's truncated source increment and uses ONE/ONE;
its independently blended alpha is unchanged. No destination snapshot or
float-emulation path is added. CPU-prepared input-alpha rows retain cutout,
zero-alpha replacement and destination-colour coverage;they do not compute
the output colour. Readable masks affect only the CPU-masked presets.
Replacement's original authored empty box and physical crop remain one atomic
preparation. Their sampler/mask changes are ordered observable cuts,not
compatible batching credit. Preserved-alpha writes invalidate an earlier opaque
target certificate before a following draw can use it. New filtered presets
with positive draw alpha below2^-32 remain counted CPU numerical debt:one
subnormal-opacity max-preset witness exceeds D2 by6 levels on D3D12. No tolerance
is relaxed for this domain. Nearest and existing cheap filtered owners retain
their original rules. Qualification requires the filtered preset base and
remaining-scale/negative-source and tiny-opacity companions,nearest neighbours,unchanged hard
individual RGBA0/2 and outside coverage,typed pre-write negatives,ordinary
zero transport and exact actual-byte state controls. Synthetic accumulation
maxima are informational under D2,not real-game composition clearance. The
first-generation fractional-cutout precision fence below is retained;other
unproved source/program/equation and numerical owners remain counted debt.

The first-generation selected hardware-filter companion explicitly rejects
nonopaque source cutout with fractional magnification on both physical axes.
This is counted ordered CPU debt, not GPU execution or an accepted precision
exception. One normal/cutout control at draw alpha1 has first excess3 on
Mesa D3D12; the same control stays within2 on llvmpipe. The guard is semantic
and applies on both drivers; it is not tied to a pixel, title or camera.
Native validation and other unproved filtered presets remain pending. Existing
qualified cheap owners stay first. Admitted filtered variants retain hard
individual RGBA±2 and exact outside coverage; CPU boundaries require exact
CPU pixels and unchanged readable state. No tolerance is relaxed here.

R03-PRIM-CLASSIC-OWNED extends the older flat/outline rules only for early-Classic
opaque application rectangles. Their inherited Classic exclusion below continues
to cover other shapes and states. Filled borrowed admission is unchanged;
owned partial/inverse rectangles and all Classic GUI draws remain excluded.

R03-TILE-ORDINARY-FUNCTION extends the earlier nearest-only tile scope.
Within its paired bounds it supersedes the former blanket TILE exclusions
for ordinary presets/masks,Classic filter preferences and physical filtered
selected/cardinal cells. It does not supersede measured precision fences,
source/coordinate/preparation limits,program/depth/auxiliary owners or the
original matrices' pixel thresholds. Its finite correlated admission ratio
is not a percentage of all possible function variants or whole-game branches.

R03-SPR-NEAREST-CAPACITY also prefers the existing bounded point preparator for
large immutable rotated pictures. Prepared row metadata grows geometrically;
all resource versions and device geometry validate before any sprite prefix
executes. A preparation allocation failure or signed-coordinate overflow still
rejects before enqueue. The larger atomic budget is not a shader or colour
arithmetic change,does not extend other families and does not remove the
unchanged8192 ordinary primitive limit.

The same rule's resident sample-row port also keeps signed64 fixed20
coordinates for its CPU-only UV preparator. Its only product consumer is
the ordinary rotated full-sprite mode21 packet:resident_nearest_sample_rows
intersects legal nearest intervals and emits bounded hardware UVs. No fixed
coordinate reaches integer shader arithmetic. Actual shader row consumers
retain their signed32 checks;finite/int source bounds,the1048576 visible-pixel
metadata ceiling,2048 rows and65536 atomic operations remain unchanged.
The capacity-rotation-wide companion must pair3456 correlated inputs across
six policies,four target owners,world/GUI,three source-alpha classes,signed
scales,six rotations and all12 enabled/disabled presets. Existing unimplemented
non-axis preset owners stay explicitly counted exact CPU debt,not qualified
GPU variants or final exceptions. Admitted endpoints require EXACT RGBA0;
individual blends2,outside coverage/disabled channels0,48 compatible opaque
eight-call groups,12 numbered actual-byte state seals,zero ordinary transport
and exact detached actual bytes remain gates. Repeat with the full write mask;
the existing three pixel negatives and capacity/dense/axis-wide/fog neighbours
are required. A finite coordinate companion is not whole-function coverage.

Normal nearest non-Classic sprite rotations whose CPU owner queues its work
retain a separate transient GPU metadata queue. The small binary,large axis
and full-sprite sample-row producers share that order;numbered alpha-preserving
targets keep their existing immediate owner. Packets retain immutable source
versions and the original target incarnation/extent,not the active target at
delivery. Atomic preparation can commit or roll back without a delivered
prefix. Before delivery,target versions are rebased after intervening opaque
writes. No CPU colour replay or destination snapshot is introduced.

A complete opaque unit surface copy and an opaque filled rectangle do not
flush the CPU rotation queue;the GPU preserves that distinction. Points,
explicit clears,ordinary draw barriers,public reads and scene completion do
flush it. A child clear can therefore deliver older packets to their original
parent. A known-opaque surface remains certified when the selected alpha
policy stamps255 or adds a nonnegative term with destination factorONE.
An empty additive source does not revoke that witnessed fact. Unknown alpha
is not promoted by this rule. Controlled resize/retirement finishes work for
the retiring resource;unrelated application retirement preserves the SCREEN
queue. Successful state restoration discards the old transient timeline;
unexpected loss invalidates affected volatile owners. Wire schemas are unchanged.

The existing small binary producer requires six EXACT RGBA0 sequences in
capacity-rotation-order-small,across Studio13/15/17 and both ordinary owners,
plus the three exact-pixel negatives. The wider capacity-rotation-order
companion requires50 EXACT RGBA0 sequences:
three Studio policies,two ordinary owners and eight schedules spanning wide,
small and axis sprites,opaque copies/rectangles,points,clears and public
observations. First-generation owned application targets retain their immediate
alpha-preserving CPU owner. Two modern mixed axis/rotated schedules additionally
require atomic preparation of distinct immutable sampler states. This is queued
metadata sharing,not a merger of incompatible GL draw states.
Its38 admitted ordinary sequences require zero
readback/mask/RGB/snapshot transport;12 explicit-observer sequences receive no
zero-read credit. The two former first-generation independent-SCREEN axis
CPU boundaries now require the positively witnessed byte-alpha GPU owner,
with unchanged exact RGBA/order comparisons. Six
sealed numbered states are saved/loaded/resaved with the owning EGL context
detached. Alpha-one,colour-one and outside-one injections must reject.
Resource transaction controls cover rollback,retry,uncertain delivery,context
loss,unfinished preparation,resize,retirement and old-timeline discard. Existing
capacity/dense/axis/fog matrices,coverage negatives and frontend state gates
remain requirements. Other deferred families,mutable sources and untested
interleavings remain debt;this is not whole-function or whole-game closure.

R03-SPR-LARGE-AXIS-PACKET's SCREEN CPU-metadata consumer may retain signed64 fixed20
coordinates in its existing row fields. It turns them into bounded hardware
nearest UV intervals before submission;no such coordinate enters a shader.
The ordinary finite/source bounds,row capacity and signed32 shader consumers
are unchanged. Its paired capacity-axis-wide companion covers72 modern
SCREEN/unstacked-application world/GUI controls with a3072x256 arithmetic
crop,reflections,trim,alpha/masks/normal/add/inverse and disabled blending;
its640x480 destination includes partial alpha with an explicitly revoked
opacity certificate as well as actual opaque backgrounds.
repeat with the full write mask. EXACT endpoints and opaque eight-call groups
remain RGBA0,individual blends2,outside coverage/disabled channels exact,
zero ordinary observations/snapshots/replay and actual detached bytes.
An ordinary nearest interval uses a2^-7 texel interior margin at a certified
near-boundary,strictly below the0.01 guard for every other boundary. The
previous0.001 margin could sample the preceding texel in a wide raw-copy
control;this is a discrete sampler correction,not blend tolerance. Both
previous capacity/dense and fog profiles remain required neighbours.
Preserved/numbered,Classic/first-generation,rotated and filtered wide-source
domains do not gain qualification from this modern SCREEN companion.

An unqualified wide-source axis companion exposed a first failing green-channel
difference55 in the older first-generation independent point packet. This is
open correctness debt,not an accepted D2 precision exception. The separate
independent-point/axis expansion remains withheld;the CPU-only rotated
sample-row extension does not certify that different policy/source-span
domain. CPU pixel bodies are
unchanged;the observation is not yet attributed by a baseline binary pair.

R03-SPR-NEAREST-CAPACITY also prefers the existing bounded point preparator for
positive first-generation unfiltered non-reciprocal axis reductions at the public unmasked
entry,matching the masked entry's existing preference. The old float-phase
sampler can promote a binary64 nearest tie into the adjacent texel. Full-mask
authored controls must keep these cheap opaque samples EXACT0; this does not
relax D2 or replace a CPU writer. Qualified reciprocal copies retain their
original preference. Unsupported large-axis,filter,special-state
and other generation paths retain their original owners. Shader bodies remain
unchanged; the existing point packets own tint,coverage and fixed blending.
The full-mask companion also observes the unchanged modern numbered predecessor
by its actual packet and reports its at-most-one GPU-local snapshot separately.
It remains existing D8 debt,not new capacity-route or zero-snapshot credit.

R03-SPR-DESTINATION-FACTORS qualifies ordinary packaged nearest full/part sprites
for inverse-destination-alpha/destination-alpha and inverse-destination-colour/zero
fixed-function blending. The unchanged CPU point/rotation writers remain the
reference. Common sprite preparation reuses existing bounded point/rotation
geometry and integer tint; typed neutral factors select OpenGL separate blending
without a destination snapshot or arithmetic emulator. Alpha-preserving targets
blend coverage; opaque targets use the existing opaque integer shader variant
and write alpha in the same draw, so subsequent overlapping fragments observe
the correct destination. Disabled channels stay exact, and the public part leaf
honours its existing factor-specific mask while its other mask semantics stay
unchanged. Qualified disabled copies/no-writes are EXACT RGBA0; individual
factor blends are TOLERANCE±2 with exact outside coverage. The existing neutral
sprite-function-matrix destination-factors profile requires1728 correlated
six-policy/four-owner world/GUI pairs with trim/sign/scale/rotation/source-alpha/
cutout/mask axes. The part companion retains7056 vectors,explicit ordered CPU
boundaries,36 numbered actual-byte states and both part/general aliases.
Both require source reuse,compatible batching,zero ordinary reads/masks/queries/
snapshots/replay,exact sealed actual bytes and rejecting comparator negatives.
Runtime/spine/nine-slice sources,filtered factor writers,custom programs/equations,
fog/depth/auxiliary planes and existing geometry/capacity bounds remain explicit
unqualified CPU debt. No CPU raster,state schema,whole-game or speed claim.

R03-SPR-PART-EMPTY also preserves either finite zero axis across ordinary
targets/policies/states: both CPU source owners return before sampling. The
sprite-part-function zero-scale profile pairs the complete7056-vector matrix,
requiring EXACT unchanged bytes and zero visible drawing/transport credit for
ordinary no-ops,while active programs retain their existing ordered boundary.
The resource contract additionally proves unchanged logical version,authority
and queued prefix across both aliases and all twelve blend selectors. This
does not widen visible geometry or specialized source/program admission.


### Ordinary full-sprite axis variants

The existing full-sprite generator's `axis-function` companion spans3456
correlated vectors:six policies,four independent owners,world/GUI,all twelve
presets,source alpha,draw opacity,tint,masks,cutout,trim,reflection and axes
1/16..64. Classic retains nearest policy with interpolation requested;
Studio full-axis colour uses hardware filtering and fixed blending. The
preparator shares existing typed input-alpha/coverage rows for sub-eighth,
cutout and nonopaque-support decisions. It does not rasterise colour or read
the destination. Old cheap additive packets retain priority where already
qualified. Replacement reuses the authored empty-box/crop transaction;each
ordered overlapping pair remains an observable batch cut,not a per-sprite
snapshot or an arbitrary flush. Every new packet keeps the full-sprite mask.

The filtered-axis strict slice requires3456 GPU controls,EXACT0/individual2,
exact outside/disabled channels,zero ordinary reads/masks/queries/replay or
new snapshots,864 exact numbered states and detached actual bytes. No-op
admissions are separately counted. `axis-point-function-audit` inventories
nearest axes without granting CPU or inherited-predecessor work GPU credit;
the strict point profile rejects either. `function-rotation-audit` and
`function-point-rotation-audit` retain full D2/coverage/state gates on admitted
packets and count all remaining CPU cases. The generator's existing explicit
predecessor-debt diagnostic remains qualification NONE,not a pixel waiver
for a new packet. Neither these finite matrices nor their admitted percentage
establish a complete function denominator or whole-game qualification.

Source/anchor/projected-extent and row-capacity bounds remain. New filtered
preset/support opacity below2^-32 and the shared first-generation variable-
alpha positive-cutout fractional-magnification boundary stay counted CPU.
Remaining nearest preparation,rotated presets/replacement,nonconstant runtime
sources,Spine/nine-slice/custom programs,depth and unqualified special states
retain their existing owner or explicit debt;they are not blanket precision
exceptions. The nearest audit also exposes retained normal numbered-target
predecessor snapshots separately. CPU pixel bodies/defaults and state schemas
are unchanged. The resource fixture pairs newly admitted cutout no-ops with
a separately allocated CPU renderer;fake-device silence is not its oracle.
The GPU contract covers all sixteen valid axis masks and rejects malformed16
before the complete clear/draw prefix on both API families.

The R03-SPR-NEAREST-FUNCTION physical-axis range is extended from1/8..8 to
1/8..64 by the shared producer and device validator together. Qualification
requires the sprite-function-matrix wide-scales profile:3456 correlated
complete RGBA pairs spanning all six policies, four ordinary owners, world/GUI,
normal/replacement/inverse/additive states, masks, trim, tint, source/draw alpha,
axis/rotation and both synthetic layouts. Each physical axis covers every integer
scale1..64 plus representative fractional wide scales. Existing projected-area,
geometry, region-count and source fences remain unchanged, including Studio's
separate large-axis CPU kernel. Rejecting comparator negatives and affected
partials remain mandatory; an admission witness alone is not pixel qualification.

The rectangle function matrix supersedes the older rectangle rules' target,
generation, GUI, gradient and outline exclusions for normal full-write state with
an on-demand device owner. Their already-qualified execution bodies and controls
remain intact. Non-normal/custom draw state, phase planes and authored bounds

| R03-SPR-SCALE-BOUNDS | EXACT RGBA0 nearest opaque/binary endpoints and unchanged coverage/channels; TOLERANCE±2 individual blended/filtered draws | Existing rotated point geometry resolves source cells before the ordinary integer GPU packet; full-axis filtered SPRITE uses the existing physical atlas support rows and fixed blending. CPU pixel writers and shaders are unchanged. | Packaged rotated point scales2^-14..16384 retain clipped-area/source/2048-region fences. Axis point and runtime-source scale bounds are unchanged.
